C. KDPG is converted into oxaloacetate and carbon dioxide. - This doesn&#x27;t correspond to any known KDPG reaction.

D. KDPG is cleaved into D-glyceraldehyde 3-phosphate and pyruvate. - This is exactly the reaction catalyzed by KDPG aldolase (2-keto-3-deoxy-6-phosphogluconate aldolase), EC 4.1.2.14.

The answer is D.
